To enable computing correlations across target languages, we propose a PMI-based translation metric, which is less dependent on the target language and correlates strongly with chrF. We find that CLA with English predicts translation quality comparably to or better than source-target CLA, providing new evidence that LLMs use English as an internal pivot language.

</details>


### [173] [Probing Character-level Transformers for the Spanish L-shaped Morphome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03452)
*Akhilesh Kakolu Ramarao,Kevin Tang,Wiebke Petersen,Dinah Baer-Henney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过探针实验证明，字符级Transformer不仅能够复现西班牙语L形形态（morphome）的不规则交替模式，还编码了该形态类别本身（而非仅可见的表层交替）。这种编码位于中间解码器的词干末尾辅音位置，且具有词项特异性——模型学到的具体动词比架构选择更重要，但其泛化方式与人类不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明字符级Transformer能复现西班牙语L形形态这一复杂不规则模式，但这些证据仅描述模型产生什么输出，而未揭示模型内部表征了什么。作者旨在探明模型是仅仅学会表象，还是真正编码了形态类别本身。

Method: 对五种架构、每种训练12个模型（共60个模型），在词元分离（lemma-disjoint）交叉验证下进行探测分析，并设置控制组和表面基线（surface baselines）对照。考察L形类别的可解码性、对同形词干的稳健性、迁移能力，以及编码的具体位置和架构/词项的方差来源。

Result: 1) L形类别在所有表面基线之上均可解码；2) 即使所有形式共享同一词干（无可见交替）也能解码；3) 用交替实例训练的探针仍能对非交替实例分类；4) 编码定位在中间解码器词干末尾辅音位置，先于交替形式被读出；5) 词项特异性起主导作用——模型学了哪些动词比模型架构更重要。

Conclusion: 模型将L形形态存储为词项特异性的词汇抽象（item-specific lexical abstraction），足以复现该模式，但无法像人类一样将其泛化。这与人类将形态视为规则性概括的方式存在本质差异。

Abstract: When a transformer learns an irregular morphological pattern, what has it learned? Our test case is the Spanish \emph{L-shaped morphome}, a complex irregular pattern in which the verb's stem alternates in exactly the first-person singular indicative and all subjunctive forms, and whose membership no phonological, semantic, or syntactic feature predicts. Prior studies have shown that character-level transformers can reproduce this pattern, but that evidence describes what models produce, not what they represent. Probing five architectures, twelve trained models each, under lemma-disjoint cross-validation with controls and surface baselines, we show that the models encode the L-shaped class itself, not just its visible alternations. It is decodable above every surface baseline, survives instances in which every form shows the same stem, and probes trained on alternating instances still classify non-alternating ones. The encoding is localized where the stem choice is made, at the stem-final consonant position of the middle decoder, before the alternant is read. And it is item-specific: which verbs a model learned matters far more than which architecture it is. The models store the morphome as an item-specific lexical abstraction, sufficient to reproduce the pattern but not to generalize it as humans do.

</details>


### [174] [Efficient Multilingual Neural Machine Translation via Corpus-Driven Vocabulary Pruning: An English-Arabic Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03480)
*Ahmed Amine Aliane,Nasredine Semmar,Hassina Aliane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用优化框架，结合词汇表剪枝方法与针对性微调策略，用于压缩多语言神经机器翻译（MNMT）模型。在M2M100、NLLB-200和mBART-50三个模型上，针对英语-阿拉伯语语言对进行评估，将词汇表从超过128,000个token缩减至约10,000个，实现60%的内存节省且无性能损失，剪枝后的模型甚至可媲美或超越专用双语模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练多语言模型在神经机器翻译中面临过大词汇表和嵌入层带来的内存与计算开销问题。尽管已有剪枝、量化和知识蒸馏等压缩方法，但它们主要保留原始词汇表结构，未能解决词汇表本身过大这一低效根源。

Method: 提出一个通用优化框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）词汇表剪枝方法，将词汇表从超过128,000个token缩减至约10,000个token；（2）针对性的微调协议，在剪枝后对模型进行优化以恢复或提升翻译性能。该框架在M2M100、NLLB-200和mBART-50三个模型上验证，使用英语-阿拉伯语语言对。

Result: 词汇表从128,000+缩减至约10,000个token，实现60%内存节省且无性能损失。剪枝并微调后的M2M100在BLEU上达到42.04（对照双语模型OPUS-MTen-ar的44.59），但在COMET指标上显著超越（0.8730 vs 0.7911），展现出更优秀的语义充分性和流畅度。

Conclusion: 通过词汇表剪枝结合定向微调，优化后的多语言模型可以在显著降低内存开销的同时匹配甚至超越专用双语模型的翻译质量，证明词汇表级别的压缩是提升MNMT模型效率的有效方向。

Abstract: The adoption of large pre-trained multilingual models for neural machine translation (MNMT) faces a major challenge: excessive memory and computational consumption due to overly large vocabularies and embedding layers. Although existing compression methods like pruning, quantization and knowledge distillation reduce parameter redundancy, they mainly preserve the structure of the original vocabulary, thereby leaving a major source of inefficiency unresolved. We propose in this paper a general optimization framework that combines a vocabulary pruning method with a targeted fine-tuning protocol for MNMT models. We evaluate the proposed framework using three models (M2M100, NLLB-200, mBART-50) on the English-Arabic language pair. Our approach reduces the vocabulary size from over 128,000 to approximately 10,000 tokens, enabling a 60% memory saving without any loss in performance. Results show that optimized multilingual models can match or exceed the performance of dedicated bilingual baselines. In particular, the pruned and fine-tuned M2M100 model achieves a competitive BLEU score of 42.04 (against 44.59 for the OPUS-MTen- ar bilingual model) while it significantly outperforms it on the COMET metric (0.8730 vs 0.7911) revealing superior semantic adequacy and fluency.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Initialization Loss: A Systematic Study of Token Embedding Initialization Strategies for LLM Vocabulary Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03494)
*Raviraj Joshi,Utkarsh Vaidya,Sanjay Singh Chauhan,Niranjan Wartikar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Vocabulary extension is an efficient way to adapt pretrained large language models (LLMs) to new languages, but the initialization of newly added token embeddings can strongly affect continued pre-training (CPT) efficiency. We present a systematic study of more than 20 initialization strategies for Hindi vocabulary extension in Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B. Our comparison spans vocabulary-averaging baselines; external and learned initialization methods, including FOCUS, top-k semantic retrieval, and residual MLP mappings; subword composition; norm calibration; and input-output asymmetry. We find that subword composition methods outperform both vocabulary averaging and external/learned initialization approaches. Within subword composition, asymmetric variants achieve the lowest observed early validation loss and reveal distinct preferences for input and output embedding initialization. The best observed configuration initializes the input embedding matrix with uniform subword averaging and Hindi-specific norm calibration, and the output language modeling head with character-length-weighted subword averaging. Relative to the standard Mean-all baseline, this full initialization pipeline reaches comparable validation loss with over a 6x reduction in CPT steps and exceeds the baseline's 3,500-step MILU-Hindi accuracy after only 500 steps. Finally, we show that initialization loss and initialization bits-per-byte (Init BPB) are unreliable predictors of downstream convergence, whereas lightweight CPT, as few as 50 steps, provides a cost-effective and reliable signal for selecting the best initialization strategy.

</details>


### [176] [ConlangBench: Exploring Language Knowledge and Learning in LLMs through Diverse Constructed Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03505)
*Jinhong Jeong,Seungyeop Yi,Sangah Lee,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ConlangBench，首个大规模人工语言（conlang）基准数据集，包含21种人工语言、2100万条人工语言-英语平行句对及32.1万词汇条目。通过双向翻译实验发现：模型在后验式人工语言（词汇源于自然语言）上表现更好；在训练实验中，模型能学习所有具备充足平行语料的8种人工语言，但学习曲线因人工语言的构造方式而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工语言（conlang）是刻意创造的人类语言，具有丰富的语言学创造力，为研究大语言模型（LLM）的语言学习能力提供了独特视角。然而，现有的人工语言在大语言模型研究中基本未被开发，缺乏系统性的基准评估与训练资源。

Method: 构建了包含21种现有人工语言的大规模基准ConlangBench，收集超过2100万条人工语言-英语平行句对（其中20种非世界语人工语言有43万句对）以及32.1万条词汇条目。开展双向翻译实验和模型训练实验，分析不同构造类型（先验式a priori vs 后验式a posteriori）人工语言对LLM学习效果的影响。

Result: 双向翻译实验中，模型在后验式人工语言上表现更优，反映了人工语言的设计特征。训练实验表明，模型能够学习所有具备充足平行语料的8种人工语言，但学习曲线因人工语言的构造方式不同而有所差异。

Conclusion: 人工语言为研究LLM如何习得低资源语言提供了独特的试验场（testbed），ConlangBench可作为评估和训练LLM在人工语言上的基准，推动该方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: Constructed languages (conlangs) are intentionally created human languages with a rich tradition of linguistic creativity. Despite their potential for studying language learning in large language models (LLMs), existing conlangs remain largely underexplored in LLM research. We present ConlangBench, the first large-scale benchmark for evaluating and training LLMs on 21 existing conlangs. We collect over 21M conlang-English parallel sentence pairs (including 430K pairs across the 20 non-Esperanto conlangs) and 321K vocabulary entries. In bidirectional translation experiments, we find that models perform better on a posteriori conlangs, whose vocabularies are derived from natural languages, reflecting the design characteristics of conlangs. Training on ConlangBench also shows that models can learn all eight conlangs for which sufficient parallel corpora are available, while their learning curves vary depending on how the conlangs were created. Our findings suggest that conlangs provide a unique testbed for investigating how LLMs acquire low-resource languages.

</details>


### [177] [ChronoLens: Measuring Language Change Across Time, Languages, and Linguistic Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03507)
*Gagan Bhatia,Julian Schlenker,Simone Paolo Ponzetto,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoLens框架，结合冻结多语言语言模型、特征对齐交叉编码器（crosscoders）和事后语言学干预，在统一分析空间中测量44.98百万文档（约17.2亿token，涵盖五个议会传统、1803-2026年）的形态、句法、语义和语用层面的历史语言变化，发现这些层面在同一语言内变化幅度相当，但不同语言在变化时间、幅度和方向上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史语言变化涉及形态、句法、语义和语用等多个层面，但现有计算研究通常使用不兼容的表示方式来分别考察这些层面，因此无法确定它们是否在不同语言中协同演化。作者希望在一个统一的分析空间中同时考察变化幅度和方向，以揭示跨层面、跨语言、跨历史时期的结构化语言演化规律。

Method: 提出ChronoLens框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 冻结多语言语言模型提供基础表示；(2) 特征对齐的交叉编码器（feature-aligned crosscoders）将表示转化为稀疏特征；(3) 事后语言学干预（post-hoc linguistic interventions）用于定位和量化特定语言学层面的变化。将该框架应用于五个议会语料库，涵盖1803至2026年共约44.98百万文档和17.2亿token。

Result: 稀疏表示与语言学统计量的一致性显著优于稠密嵌入和池化稀疏自编码器（ρ=0.72 vs 0.29和0.28）。发现形态、句法、语义和语用在单一语言内变化幅度大致相当，但不同语言在变化的时间、幅度和方向上差异显著。相似的幅度可能掩盖不同的演化轨迹。

Conclusion: 历史语言变化是一个结构化的多维过程：仅测量变化距离不足以刻画语言演化，跨语言的有意义比较需要同时考虑变化距离和方向两个维度。这为计算历史语言学提供了统一的分析框架。

Abstract: Historical language change affects morphology, syntax, semantics, and pragmatics, yet computational studies typically examine these levels with incompatible representations and therefore cannot determine whether they evolve together across languages. We address this problem by asking how the magnitude and direction of change vary across linguistic levels, languages, and historical periods within a single analytical space. We introduce ChronoLens, a framework that combines frozen multilingual language models, feature-aligned crosscoders, and post-hoc linguistic interventions, and apply it to 44.98 million documents and approximately 17.2 billion tokens from five parliamentary traditions spanning 1803--2026. The resulting sparse representations agree substantially more strongly with linguistic statistics than dense embeddings or a pooled sparse autoencoder ($ρ=0.72$ versus $0.29$ and $0.28$), and reveal that morphology, syntax, semantics, and pragmatics generally change by comparable amounts within a language, while languages differ markedly in when, how far, and in which direction they change. These findings show that historical language change is a structured, multidimensional process: similar magnitudes can conceal different trajectories, and meaningful cross-linguistic comparison requires measuring both distance and direction.

</details>


### [178] [Consensus Measures for Unstructured Biomedical Text Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03529)
*Pascal Wullschleger,Christian Kreis,Martin A. Walter,Marc Pouly,Jennifer Foster*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究生物医学文献标注任务中开放式文本标签的软性评分者间信度量化问题，比较了多种语义等价度量方法，并提出基于自然语言推理的度量作为折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学文献挖掘常需要开放式标签（目标概念预先未知），但这类标签的一致性或信度难以量化，传统的硬性一致性指标不适用。

Method: 通过合成实验评估多种语义等价度量方法（包括嵌入向量方法、大规模语言模型方法、自然语言推理方法）在软性评分者间信度估计中的表现，分析各方法的失效模式。

Result: 嵌入方法可扩展性好但难以区分相似但不同的概念；大规模语言模型效果有前景但受限于计算可扩展性，难以用于估计随机一致性；自然语言推理方法被推荐为合理的折中方案。

Conclusion: 软性评分者间信度可以通过多种语义等价度量来量化，但度量选择会影响估计的失效模式；自然语言推理方法在效果与可扩展性之间取得平衡，是值得推荐的选择。

Abstract: Biomedical literature is increasingly mined for knowledge beyond the questions it was written to answer. Because the target concepts are not known in advance, annotators prefer open-ended labels, whose agreement is hard to quantify. We study soft inter-rater reliability for annotators providing unstructured texts for biomedical annotation tasks. Synthetic experiments show that soft reliability can be quantified using a variety of semantic equivalence measures, and that the choice of measure affects failure modes of the estimation. Embeddings are scalable, but limited when differentiating similar but distinct concepts. Large language models are promising, but limited by scalability for estimating agreement by chance. Finally, we suggest measures based on natural language inference as a sensible compromise.

</details>


### [179] [Cross-Lingual Bias in Large Language Models: A Comparative Analysis of English and Swahili](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03532)
*Ruolei Zhang,Teddy Njuguna,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过4,900对对称的英语-斯瓦希里语提示对，对GPT-5.2和Gemini 2.5 Flash在九个社会偏见轴上进行评估（共19,600个补全文本），发现社会偏见在跨语言环境中会发生"转化"而非"转移"——刻板印象率最高变化12个百分点，Gemini中性情感率在斯瓦希里语中翻倍，GPT-5.2在英语中拒绝169条提示但在斯瓦希里语中零拒绝，且超过55%的提示对在两个模型中产生语义不相似的补全结果，证明仅基于英语的偏见审计不足以覆盖多语言部署场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地部署在多种语言环境中，但安全对齐与偏见评估仍以英语为中心。现有研究未充分探究社会偏见是否能跨语言泛化，以及英语单语偏见审计是否足以保障多语言部署的安全性。

Method: 向GPT-5.2和Gemini 2.5 Flash提交4,900对对称的英语-斯瓦希里语提示对，覆盖九个社会人口偏见轴，共获得19,600条模型补全文本，并从刻板印象发生率、情感极性、拒绝行为及跨语言语义相似性四个维度进行系统评估。

Result: 研究发现偏见在跨语言场景中发生转化而非简单转移：特定偏见轴的刻板印象率偏移高达12个百分点；Gemini在斯瓦希里语中的中性情感率翻倍；GPT-5.2在英语中拒绝169条提示但在斯瓦希里语中零拒绝，表明拒绝行为在行为层面锚定于英语表层形式；超过55%的提示对在两个模型中产生语义不相似的补全。

Conclusion: 仅依赖英语的偏见审计无法为多语言部署提供充分覆盖，安全评估框架必须将多语言维度纳入标准实践。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multilingual contexts, yet safety alignment and bias evaluation remain overwhelmingly English-centric. We investigate whether social biases generalise across languages by submitting 4,900 symmetric English--Swahili prompt pairs to GPT-5.2 and Gemini 2.5 Flash across nine demographic bias axes, yielding 19,600 completions evaluated for stereotype prevalence, sentiment, refusal behaviour, and cross-lingual semantic similarity. Our findings show that bias transforms rather than transfers: stereotype rates shifted by up to 12 percentage points on specific axes, Gemini's neutral-sentiment rate doubled in Swahili, and GPT-5.2 refused 169 prompts in English and zero in Swahili, consistent with refusal behaviour anchored to English-language surface forms at the behavioural level. Over 55% of prompt pairs produced semantically dissimilar completions across both models. These reinforce the idea that English-only bias audits do not produce adequate coverage for multilingual deployment.

</details>


### [180] [Hi-TTRL: Regulating Consensus with Hints for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03545)
*Kunbin Xu,Xingzuo Li,Xuefeng Bai,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hi-TTRL，一种测试时强化学习框架，通过提示引导（hint-guided）调节rollout群体的一致性强度（consensus strength），解决标准TTRL中多数投票产生的奖励信号对一致性强度过敏感的问题，在多个数据集和骨干模型上一致优于标准TTRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时强化学习（TTRL）通过多数投票构造伪标签来更新策略，无需标注数据。然而，多数投票产生的奖励信号对rollout群体中一致性强度（最常见答案的频率）高度敏感：低一致性会导致不可靠伪标签被不成比例的大优势放大，高一致性则导致奖励对比度降低、出现梯度消失。因此需要调节一致性强度以提升TTRL性能。

Method: Hi-TTRL首先从部分rollout群体估计一致性强度；当一致性强度超出目标区间时，调用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）提示采样器。该采样器以幂变换后的前缀分布为目标，通过有限步近似采样生成rollout前缀作为提示。通过调节幂指数，可生成锐化或展平的幂目标分布，从而将rollout一致性强度引导回目标区间。

Result: 在多个数据集和骨干模型上的实验表明，Hi-TTRL一致优于标准TTRL；消融实验和一致性引导分析验证了自适应提示引导的一致性调节方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Hi-TTRL通过自适应提示引导的一致性调节机制，有效缓解了TTRL中奖励信号对一致性强度的敏感性，显著提升了测试时强化学习的推理能力。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models without labeled data by updating the policy with pseudo-labels constructed through majority voting. While effective, the reward signal assigned from majority voting is highly sensitive to consensus strength, defined as the frequency of the most common answer within a rollout group. In TTRL, consensus strength plays a dual role: it reflects both the reliability of the pseudo-label and the distribution of advantages. Low consensus can amplify updates from unreliable pseudo-labels through disproportionately large advantages, whereas high consensus reduces reward contrast and ultimately yields vanishing gradients. In this paper, we introduce Hi-TTRL, a test-time reinforcement learning framework that utilizes hints during sampling to regulate rollout consensus strength. Hi-TTRL first estimates consensus strength from a partial rollout group. When the consensus strength falls outside a target interval, it invokes a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) hint sampler. The sampler targets the power-transformed prefix distribution and uses finite-step approximate sampling to generate rollout prefixes as hints. By tuning the power exponent, Hi-TTRL generates hints with a sharpened or flattened power target, steering rollout consensus strength toward the target interval. Experiments on multiple datasets and backbones show that Hi-TTRL consistently improves over standard TTRL, with ablations and consensus-steering analyses validating the effectiveness of adaptive hint-guided consensus regulation.

</details>


### [181] [SFT Conflicts, RL Coexists: A Theoretical and Empirical Analysis of Multi-Task Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03573)
*Kejian Zhu,Zhuoran Jin,Shangqing Tu,Hongbang Yuan,Yushi Bai,Kang Liu,Juanzi Li,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现SFT与RL在多任务推理增强中表现迥异：SFT在多阶段训练中面临严重任务冲突，而RL则能实现任务的稳定共存。作者从参数层面追踪到RL产生稀疏且近似正交的任务更新，并通过多任务梯度干扰的理论分析揭示了这一机制：SFT的干扰受梯度绝对大小（范数）限制，而RL的干扰受梯度方差限制，后者产生近似正交的优化方向。基于此提出Parallel-RL范式，解耦多任务训练，显著提升效率与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究SFT与RL在多任务推理场景下为何表现出截然不同的行为，理解RL能实现多任务稳定共存的底层机制，从而设计更高效的多任务训练范式。

Method: 首先进行多阶段训练的初步实验观察SFT与RL的任务冲突差异；其次在参数层面进行实证分析，观察两种方法下任务更新的稀疏性与正交性；然后通过多任务梯度干扰的理论分析解释RL低干扰的机制，指出SFT干扰受范数限制而RL干扰受方差限制；最后基于此理论提出Parallel-RL解耦多任务训练框架。

Result: 证实了RL产生的任务更新是稀疏且近似正交的，而SFT在多任务训练中遭受严重的梯度干扰；理论上说明RL的干扰上界由优势归一化和on-policy优化导致的梯度方差所限定，该小方差界产生近似正交的优化方向；提出的Parallel-RL范式在多任务训练中显著提升了效率与灵活性。

Conclusion: SFT与RL在多任务推理中的差异根源在于梯度干扰的不同性质——SFT是范数受限，RL是方差受限。这一发现不仅从理论上解释了RL在多任务中的优势，还催生了Parallel-RL这一更高效的解耦训练范式，为大规模LLM的多任务训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) exhibit fundamentally different behaviors in enhancing multi-task reasoning for large language models (LLMs). Our preliminary experiments revealed a phenomenon: SFT suffers from severe task conflicts under multi-stage training, whereas RL enables stable coexistence across diverse tasks. Empirically, we trace this to the parameter level, observing that RL induces sparse and approximately orthogonal updates across tasks. We provide a theoretical explanation for this mechanism by analyzing multi-task gradient interference. Our results reveal a distinction: interference in SFT is norm-limited, scaling with the absolute gradient magnitude, whereas interference in RL is variance-limited, bounded by the gradient variance induced by advantage normalization and on-policy optimization. This small variance bound yields near-orthogonal optimization directions across tasks. Leveraging this insight, we propose Parallel-RL, a paradigm that decouples multi-task training, significantly improving efficiency and flexibility.

</details>


### [182] [Looking under the Wrong Lamppost: On the Limitations of Automated Translation Quality Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03577)
*Serge Gladkoff,Angelika Vaasa,Sue Ellen Wright,Ingemar Strandvik,Lifeng Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从理论与实证角度审视了翻译质量估计（QE）技术的根本局限性，指出当前QE系统在结构上不适合作独立工具用于真实翻译流程，并建议未来应转向基于MQM的自动化人工评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着QE工具和技术的快速涌现，相关研究在稳健性、透明性和可复现性方面存在明显不足，缺乏深入的关键审视。作者认为这一差距值得严格批判，因此系统性地考察QE技术的根本局限。

Method: 从理论层面剖析QE在孤立片段层面评估翻译质量的根本问题，并结合实证研究证据，总结其在泛化能力、系统偏见、过拟合、分布坍缩、性能差距、错误标注和数据稀缺等方面的多重局限。

Result: 研究发现QE存在多种相互关联且基本未解决的局限性：片段级评价忽视语篇连贯性、风格及修辞特征；实证层面存在泛化失败、系统性偏见、过拟合与分布坍缩、性能差距、错误标注困难和数据稀缺等问题。这些是源于人类语言复杂性及翻译作为认知与交际行为的结构性局限，更多数据和更好的架构均未能克服。

Conclusion: 片段级QE分数不应作为生产中路由、发布或审查跳过的独立依据；未来研究方向应聚焦于以MQM（多维质量指标）为基础的自动化人工评价。

Abstract: Automation of Translation Quality Estimation (QE) has emerged as a widely discussed approach to managing translation quality at scale, and a growing number of tools and technologies have been released in pursuit of this goal. However, the proliferation of new QE systems has not always been accompanied by robust, transparent, and reproducible research and testing. This gap deserves critical scrutiny. This paper examines some fundamental limitations of the QE technology from both theoretical and empirical perspectives, arguing that current QE systems are structurally ill-equipped to serve as reliable standalone tools in real-world translation workflows. The reviewed evidence suggests that QE suffers from a range of interrelated and largely unresolved limitations. Most fundamentally, the evaluation of the quality of translation at the level of isolated segments is problematic because it tends to miss out on cohesion, coherence, and stylistic and rhetorical text features. In addition, empirical research documents several other limitations and flaws, including failure to generalize, systematic biases, overfitting and distribution collapse, performance gaps, error annotation challenges, and data scarcity. These are structural limitations arising from the complexity of human language and translation as a cognitive and communicative act - limitations that more data and better architectures have so far not overcome. Consequently, segment-level QE scores should not be used as a standalone basis for routing, release, or review bypass in production; we argue future work should focus on automating human evaluation grounded in MQM.

</details>


### [183] [Disentangling Language Modeling and Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03599)
*Mykola Haltiuk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出字节级语言模型具有结构性优势：任意两个字节级模型共享输出空间，知识迁移无需考虑分词器差异。作者假设字节级模型的两种分布（下一字节分布与patch边界分布）可近乎独立地解耦与调整，并提出两个实验加以验证，呼吁社区采用字节级接口作为共享标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统论证字节级语言模型主要关注鲁棒性、多语言公平性和字符级技能，但本文指出一个被忽视的结构性优点：字节级模型之间共享输出空间，知识迁移是精确且与分词方式无关的，从而解决当前基于tokenizer的迁移障碍。

Method: 作者提出假设：字节级模型产生的两种分布（下一字节分布和能力相关，patch边界分布和分词相关）可以解耦并几乎独立地改变。文中设计了两个关键实验：(1) 模型吸收教师能力而保持自身边界；(2) 模型改变边界放置方式而保持能力。并提供了支撑该假设的初步测量。

Result: 本文尚未完成最终实验，主要贡献为提出假设、设计两个验证实验，并提供初步测量数据作为支持性证据。

Conclusion: 作者主张社区应转向字节级接口作为共享标准。若假设成立，一旦字节级模型成为常态，在模型间迁移能力和重塑边界将变得廉价且常规，摆脱当前每个模型独立tokenizer的阻碍。

Abstract: Byte-level language models are usually argued for on the grounds of robustness, multilingual fairness, and character-level skills. We point to a different, structural advantage: because they read and write bytes, any two of them share an output space, so knowledge transfer between them is exact and independent of how either was originally tokenized. We hypothesize that the two distributions a byte-level model produces, one over the next byte, one over where its patch boundaries fall, can be disentangled and changed almost independently. A model could absorb a teacher's capability while keeping its own boundaries, or change how it places those boundaries while keeping its capabilities. We lay out the two experiments that would settle the hypothesis, alongside preliminary measurements of the properties they rest on. We argue that the community should move toward a byte-level interface as a shared standard: if the hypothesis holds, then once byte-level models are the norm, transferring capabilities and reshaping boundaries between them become cheap and routine, free of the per-model tokenizer that blocks them today.

</details>


### [184] [Language-Specialized Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Multilingual LLM-Based ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03610)
*Yuan Xie,Jiaqi Song,Xianliang Wang,Ming Lei,Jie Gao,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语言专精多教师在线策略蒸馏（LS-MOPD）方法，将多语言ASR中的语言专精知识获取与多语言能力整合解耦：先用强化学习独立优化各语言专精教师，再通过语言路由和token级多教师蒸馏将知识整合到通用多语言学生模型中，从而减少跨语言优化冲突。在普通话、方言、粤语和英语基准上超越RL基线，甚至优于所有最佳RL教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多语言ASR系统虽具备多语言能力，但不同语言在声学、音系和词汇特征上的异质性会导致联合建模时产生优化冲突，削弱各语言的特化性能。

Method: LS-MOPD将语言专精知识获取与多语言能力整合解耦为两阶段：(1) 各语言专精教师通过强化学习独立优化；(2) 通过语言路由和token级多教师蒸馏，将教师专长整合进通用多语言学生模型。同时探索静态和动态两种声学前缀配置，研究教师-学生前缀一致性对在线策略蒸馏效果的影响。

Result: 在覆盖普通话、普通话次方言、粤语和英语的基准上，LS-MOPD显著优于RL基线，并稳定超越由最优RL教师定义的实证性能包络，展示出超越所有教师的多语言ASR泛化潜力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦语言专精学习与多语言整合，LS-MOPD有效规避了跨语言优化冲突，实现超越单个最优教师的通用多语言ASR性能。

Abstract: Modern LLM-based ASR systems have established multilingual capability as a standard feature, leveraging large-scale multilingual corpora and LLMs' cross-lingual knowledge to achieve competitive performance across multilingual benchmarks. However, joint modeling of languages with heterogeneous acoustic, phonological, and lexical characteristics inevitably introduces optimization conflicts, undermining language-wise specialization. To address this challenge, we propose Language-Specialized Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (LS-MOPD), which decouples language-specific knowledge acquisition from multilingual capability integration: language-specialized teachers are independently optimized via reinforcement learning (RL), after which their expertise is integrated into a generalist multilingual student through language routing and token-level multi-teacher distillation, thereby reducing direct cross-lingual optimization conflicts. We further explore two acoustic-prefix configurations, static and dynamic, to examine how teacher--student prefix consistency influences the efficacy of on-policy distillation. Experiments on benchmarks covering Mandarin, Mandarin subdialects, Cantonese, and English demonstrate that LS-MOPD substantially outperforms RL baselines and consistently surpasses the empirical performance envelope defined by best-performing RL teachers, revealing its potential to generalize beyond all teachers in multilingual ASR.

</details>


### [185] [A machine-readable catalogue of the Tsiolkovsky papers (fond 555, Archive of the Russian Academy of Sciences), and a way to measure how well its handwriting can be read](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03617)
*Vladimir Beskorovainyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了齐奥尔科夫斯基私人档案（fond 555）的机器可读目录、断代、手写/打字页面分类及机器转录语料库，并提出一种在无地面真值档案中评估手写文本识别准确率的方法：通过对比同一文本的手稿与其打字副本的两次转录来隔离识别误差，并验证了该方法在无偏差和页面排序方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俄罗斯科学院档案馆保存的齐奥尔科夫斯基私人档案虽已数字化扫描发布，但缺乏可查询目录、全文检索和数据集，只能逐页浏览，无法进行系统的文本分析或大规模研究，亟需构建结构化的机器可读资源和可靠的识别质量评估手段。

Method: 构建涵盖全部2,019个文件和51,008页扫描的机器可读目录，从档案描述中提取1,969个文件的断代信息，对所有扫描进行手写/打字页面分类，并对322个文件（5,454页扫描）进行机器转录。评估方法上，利用打字机时代档案常同时保留手稿和打字稿的特点，对两者分别转录后对比，在同源同管线条件下隔离识别误差，并用有出版版本的样本对照地面真值验证。

Result: 在294对（27个文件）手写页面的两次阅读中，单词一致率中位数为37%；在对照真实内容的两个文件中，估计方法无偏差（偏差在一个百分点以内），且页面排序与真实情况相关（忠实版本下秩相关系数为0.92）。同一作品的两个变体仅共享19%的单词，低于单页两次阅读的一致率，说明在该质量下无法逐词对照校勘版本。

Conclusion: 该归档资源与评估方法为无地面真值的档案提供了可用的识别质量度量手段，且方法对特定应用场景（如版本校勘）的局限性给出了明确边界，并将该约束内置于工具中，负面结果被如实报告。

Abstract: The personal archive of Konstantin Tsiolkovsky (1857-1935) is held as fond 555 of the Archive of the Russian Academy of Sciences. The archive scanned the fond and published the images, but with no queryable catalogue, no full-text search and no dataset: the holdings can only be browsed one page at a time. This paper describes a machine-readable catalogue of all 2,019 files and 51,008 scans, a dating for 1,969 files taken from the archive's own descriptions, a page-level classification of every scan into handwriting and typescript, and a growing corpus of machine transcriptions (currently 322 files, 5,454 scans).
  It also reports a way to measure handwritten-text-recognition accuracy in an archive with no ground truth. Archives of the typewriter era often preserve one text twice, as manuscript and as a typed copy; transcribing both and comparing isolates the reading error, since source and pipeline are identical and only page difficulty differs. Across 294 such pairs from 27 files, two readings of a handwritten page agree on a median 37% of words. On two files that also have a published edition the estimate can be checked against ground truth: it is unbiased to within a percentage point and ranks pages as the truth does (rank correlation 0.92 where the edition is a faithful witness). This bounds use: two variants of one work here share 19% of words, below the rate at which two readings of a single page agree, so the redactions cannot be collated word by word at this quality. That negative result is reported as such, and the constraint is built into the tool.

</details>


### [186] [LoopMTP: A looped transformer guided by latent multi-token prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03624)
*Behzad Shomali,Markus Frey,David Berghaus,Joachim Koehler,Mehdi Ali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LoopMTP方法，通过将循环Transformer的循环次数与多token预测（MTP）建立结构对应关系，将第t个循环的隐藏状态与t步前token的嵌入进行软对齐，以提供密集的前瞻性监督，从而解决循环模型中的潜在过度思考与无差别计算问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过重用单层堆栈多次迭代，在固定参数下获得更深的有效深度和更强的推理能力，但现有方法缺乏跨循环的中间表示监督，导致潜在过度思考（latent overthinking）和缺乏区分度的计算（undifferentiated computation），限制其推理上限。

Method: LoopMTP将循环Transformer与多token预测（MTP）相结合：模型循环T次可以前瞻T个未来token，将第t个循环的隐藏状态与t步前token的嵌入进行软对齐（soft alignment），同时引入轻量级门控（lightweight gate）以跨迭代保留有用信息，从而提供密集的前瞻性监督。

Result: 相比非循环基线，LoopMTP在准确率上平均提升最多8.1%（相对值），且训练在多达15个循环时保持稳定。

Conclusion: 结构化的循环-预测对应能够为循环Transformer提供有效的监督信号，在不增加参数的前提下提升推理性能，并保持训练稳定性，表明MTP是弥补循环模型逐层监督缺失的有效机制。

Abstract: Looped transformers have emerged as a parameter-efficient alternative to scaling depth for strong reasoning. By reusing one stack of layers across $T$ iterations, they attain the effective depth and reasoning capabilities of larger models at a fixed parameter count. Yet existing approaches suffer from latent overthinking and undifferentiated computation, largely because intermediate representations receive no guidance across loops. Multi-token prediction (MTP) supplies exactly the dense, forward-looking supervision the loop is missing. We propose \textsc{LoopMTP}, which links the two through a structural correspondence in latent space: a model that loops $T$ times can anticipate $T$ future tokens. \textsc{LoopMTP} realizes this by softly aligning the hidden state of loop $t$ with the embedding of the token $t$ steps ahead, while a lightweight gate preserves useful information across iterations. \textsc{LoopMTP} improves average accuracy by up to 8.1\% (relative) over the non-looped baseline, with training remaining stable for up to 15 loops.

</details>


### [187] [Decoupling Generation and Selection for Budget-Constrained Faithful Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03655)
*Zeyu Wang,Guanghua Wang,Meng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化的"生成-选择"框架用于有句子预算约束的摘要生成。预训练生成器产生多个候选摘要后，组合选择器在显式预算下平衡相关性、事实性和冗余度来构建最终摘要，无需重训生成器即可支持MMR、ILP和DPP目标函数，在多个数据集上提升事实性但降低参考重叠分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抽象式摘要模型仍存在事实不一致、冗余和长度控制薄弱等问题。作者旨在通过解耦生成与选择两个阶段，提供一种模型无关的机制来改进事实性接地（factual grounding），同时解决句子预算约束下的摘要质量问题。

Method: 框架包含两阶段：1) 生成阶段：预训练生成器产生多个候选完整摘要，随后分解为句子级候选；2) 选择阶段：组合选择器在显式句子预算约束下，通过平衡相关性、事实性和冗余度三个目标构建最终摘要。支持三种目标函数：MMR（最大边际相关）、ILP（整数线性规划）、DPP-inspired的log-determinant目标，均无需重训生成器。

Result: 在CNN/DailyMail、Multi-News、FaithBench和TofuEval上取得一致的事实性和来源接地指标提升，尤其在多文档摘要任务上表现突出，但以降低参考重叠（如ROUGE）分数为代价。人工评估显示感知的一致性、相关性、清晰度和简洁性更高，仅连贯性略有下降。

Conclusion: 将生成与选择解耦可作为模型无关的手段改进摘要的事实接地能力，为既有生成器提供了一条无需重训即可增强事实性的实用路径。

Abstract: Abstractive summarization models remain vulnerable to factual inconsistency, redundancy, and weak length control. We propose a modular generation-and-selection framework for sentence-budget-constrained summarization. A pretrained generator produces multiple candidate summaries, which are decomposed into sentence-level candidates. A combinatorial selector then constructs the final summary by balancing relevance, factuality, and redundancy under an explicit budget. The framework supports MMR, ILP, and a DPP-inspired log-determinant objective without retraining the generator. Experiments on CNN/DailyMail, Multi-News, FaithBench, and TofuEval show consistent improvements in factuality and source-grounding metrics, especially for multi-document summarization, at the cost of lower reference-overlap scores. Human evaluation further indicates higher perceived consistency, relevance, clarity, and conciseness, with a small reduction in coherence. These results show that decoupling generation from selection provides a model-agnostic mechanism for improving factual grounding. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/bcfs-D05E/.

</details>


### [188] [How Closely Do LLM Reviews Align with Human Peer Review?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03659)
*Abraham Camelo-Guerrero,Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了三个大语言模型（OpenAI GPT-5.4、Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview、Anthropic Claude Opus 4.6）与人类审稿人在ICLR 2026（300篇主题匹配的论文，均衡分为oral、poster、rejected三类）上的审稿表现。结果显示三个LLM都能区分被接受与被拒论文，但均无法复现人类对oral与poster的区分；评分模式存在提供商差异（Gemini系统性偏高），且LLM与人类在关注点上也存在差异（LLM更多指出缺少基线对比，人类更常关注计算效率问题）。研究表明广泛的决策对齐并不等同于细粒度人类判断与审稿优先级的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于生成科学审稿意见，但现有评估很少在同一受控环境下考察不同提供商是否同时与会议决策及人类审稿优先级对齐。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过跨提供商的系统性对比，检验LLM审稿在决策对齐、评分尺度使用和弱点识别主题上的表现。

Method: 研究选取300篇主题匹配的ICLR 2026投稿（oral、poster、rejected各100篇），在移除决策信息后，使用相同的指令和评分量表，让OpenAI GPT-5.4、Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview和Anthropic Claude Opus 4.6逐篇生成审稿意见，并从三个维度进行分析：宽泛与细粒度决策类别的对齐程度、推荐评分尺度的使用差异、识别弱点的主题一致性。

Result: 三个LLM均能区分被接受与被拒绝的论文，但都无法复现人类评分中oral与poster的区分。评分模式呈提供商特异性：Gemini系统性给出更高评分；OpenAI和Claude在rejected和poster论文上更接近人类，但对oral论文更为挑剔。人类与LLM审稿的关注点不同：LLM更常识别缺少基线对比的问题，而人类更常提出计算效率方面的担忧。

Conclusion: 广泛的决策级对齐（区分接受/拒绝）并不能保证与人类更细粒度的判断（如oral vs poster）或审稿优先级的对齐。这提示在将LLM用于科学审稿时，需要审视其在细粒度判断和关注点偏好上的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate scientific reviews, yet existing evaluations rarely examine whether different providers align with both conference decisions and human reviewing priorities within the same controlled setting. We compare reviews from OpenAI GPT-5.4, Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview, and Anthropic Claude Opus 4.6 with human reviews and final decisions for 300 topic-matched ICLR 2026 submissions, equally divided among oral, poster, and rejected papers. Each model reviewed every paper using identical instructions and rating scales after decision information was removed. Our study contributes a cross-provider analysis of three complementary dimensions: alignment with broad and fine-grained decision categories, differences in recommendation-scale usage, and thematic agreement in identified weaknesses. All three LLMs distinguished accepted from rejected papers, but none reproduced the oral versus poster distinction present in human ratings. Scoring patterns were provider-specific: Gemini assigned systematically higher ratings, while OpenAI and Claude were closer to humans for rejected and poster papers but more critical of oral papers. Human and LLM reviews also differed in emphasis, with LLMs more frequently identifying missing baseline comparisons and humans more often raising computational-efficiency concerns. These results show that broad decision alignment does not imply agreement with finer human judgments or reviewing priorities.

</details>


### [189] [VetScore: Risk-Weighted Fact Verification for Veterinary Long-Form QA with Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03675)
*Ivan Kartáč,Jan Tovarys,Mateusz Lango,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VetScore是一种面向兽医领域长格式问答的多步骤评估方法，通过将输出分解为独立声明、评估各声明的危害潜力及其对来源摘录的忠实度，最终计算风险调整后的总体分数。该方法在专家标注的元评估数据集上，即使使用小型评测模型也能与兽医专家高度相关，并提供多维度的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然引用摘录可以提升生成输出的可靠性和对来源的忠实度，但在兽医医学等高风险领域中，生成声明仍可能对摘录不忠实。现有方法无法充分评估这一点，需要一种考虑危害潜力的评估机制来确保生成内容的安全性。

Method: VetScore采用多步骤评估流程：首先对生成输出进行分段并将其分解为独立的声明；然后对每个声明根据其危害潜力进行评分，并评估其相对于来源摘录的忠实度；最后计算风险调整后的总体分数。作者收集了专家标注的元评估数据集，并使用多种评测模型（包括小型模型）对该方法进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，VetScore即使使用小型评测模型也能与兽医专家的评分达到高相关性，同时提供跨多个维度的可解释性。

Conclusion: VetScore为兽医领域的长格式问答提供了一种可靠的、风险感知的评估方法，兼顾了忠实度评估与危害权重，且具有良好的可解释性和模型规模适应性。

Abstract: Citation excerpts can be used to increase the reliability of generated outputs and their faithfulness to cited sources, which is especially important in high-stakes domains such as human and veterinary medicine. However, this does not guarantee that generated claims are faithful to the provided excerpts. We present VetScore, a multi-step evaluation method for veterinary long-form question answering, designed to assess how well are generated claims supported by the provided excerpts, weighing this information by each claim's harm potential. VetScore first segments the output and decomposes it into individual claims, then scores each claim with respect to its harm potential and evaluates its faithfulness to source excerpts, and finally calculates the overall risk-adjusted score. We collect an expert-annotated meta-evaluation dataset, evaluate our approach with a range of judge models, and show that it achieves high correlations with veterinary experts even with small judge models, while offering explainability across multiple dimensions.

</details>


### [190] [Predicting Deep Neural Network Training Outcomes from Early Training Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03709)
*Ranjita Naik,Anh D. Nguyen,Pankaj Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究能否仅凭单个训练运行前5个epoch的遥测数据（损失、训练准确率、梯度信噪比、权重范数增长、激活饱和快照）及其超参数，预测该运行的最终结果（测试准确率、相对性能、训练动态失败如数值发散），而无需参考其他运行。在23,788次训练运行、六种架构/数据集组合上，梯度提升树实现了R²=0.92-0.99的最终准确率回归和ROC-AUC=0.983-0.998的相对分类性能，且单个epoch后即可获得有效预测。梯度级和权重级遥测比仅使用损失和准确率曲线有统计上一致的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模超参数搜索中，大量计算被浪费在那些从最初几个epoch就注定失败的超参配置上。如果能提前预判训练运行的结果，就可以大幅节省计算资源，实现更高效的计算分配。

Method: 使用梯度提升树作为预测模型，输入特征包括训练前5个epoch的逐epoch损失、训练准确率、梯度信噪比、权重范数增长、激活饱和快照以及采样的超参数。评估三个预测任务：最终测试准确率回归、域内相对性能分类、训练动态失败预测（含数值发散）。采用完全保留的超参配置集进行评测，并进行了配对消融实验和跨架构/数据集迁移实验。

Result: 在23,788次训练运行上，最终准确率回归达到R²=0.92-0.99，相对分类达到ROC-AUC=0.983-0.998。单个epoch后即可获得有用预测。梯度级和权重级遥测提供了统计上一致的改进，但实际收益因领域而异。相似架构间迁移强，跨数据集迁移主要受准确率尺度差异限制而非底层关系丢失。

Conclusion: 早期训练遥测可作为计算分配的实际决策支持信号，但任何自动化干预仍需人类监督。

Abstract: Large hyperparameter sweeps for deep neural networks spend substantial compute on configurations that are effectively doomed from the first few epochs. We study whether a single training run's own early telemetry - per-epoch loss, training accuracy, gradient signal-to-noise ratio, weight-norm growth, and an activation-saturation snapshot - together with its sampled hyperparameters, can predict that run's eventual outcome without reference to other runs. We evaluate three prediction tasks: final test accuracy, relative performance within a domain, and training-dynamics failure, including numerical divergence. Across 23,788 training runs spanning six architecture/dataset combinations, gradient-boosted trees using only the first five epochs of telemetry achieve R^2 = 0.92-0.99 for final-accuracy regression and ROC-AUC = 0.983-0.998 for relative classification on a permanently held-out set of hyperparameter configurations. Useful prediction is already available after a single epoch. A paired ablation shows that gradient- and weight-level telemetry provides a statistically consistent improvement over loss and accuracy curves alone, although the practical gain varies by domain. Transfer is strong between similar architectures, while cross-dataset transfer is limited mainly by differences in accuracy scale rather than loss of the underlying relationship. These results suggest that early-training telemetry can provide a practical decision-support signal for compute allocation while motivating human oversight for any automated intervention.

</details>


### [191] [Detecting Hallucinations and Recovering Verified Answers in Arabic Islamic Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03720)
*Khaled Ziani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HalluScoring 2026任务2.1（伊斯兰幻觉检测与寻找真相）中的系统。该系统基于微调的google/gemma-4-12B-it模型，对阿拉伯语LLM生成的回答进行两步预测：判断是否幻觉化并从六个候选选项中选择正确答案。系统在600个问答实例上取得Macro-F1 0.928、标签准确率0.935、选项准确率0.895和综合得分0.912。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在回答伊斯兰相关问题时会生成流畅但包含难以识别的事实错误的回答，因此需要自动检测LLM生成的阿拉伯语回答是否存在幻觉，并从多个相似候选中找出经核实的正确答案，以确保宗教内容的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 系统使用共享任务提供的伊斯兰知识数据集（600个问答实例，341个幻觉和259个非幻觉回答），基于微调的google/gemma-4-12B-it模型进行训练，推理时采用确定性解码，并将生成的输出进行归一化处理以提取幻觉标签和所选选项。

Result: 幻觉检测的Macro-F1分数为0.928，标签准确率为0.935，答案选择的选项准确率为0.895，综合得分为0.912。

Conclusion: 系统在两个阶段均表现良好，但选项选择准确率较低说明从合理替代项中区分经核实答案比检测幻觉响应更具挑战性。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent responses to Islamic questions while introducing factual errors that are difficult to identify. This paper presents our system for \textsc{HalluScoring 2026} Task 2.1, \textit{Islamic Hallucination Detection and Find the Truth}. The task requires a unified two-step prediction: determining whether an Arabic answer generated by an LLM is hallucinated and selecting the verified answer from six closely related candidate options. We use the Islamic knowledge dataset provided by the shared task, which contains 600 question--answer instances, including 341 hallucinated and 259 non-hallucinated answers. Our system is based on the fine-tuned \texttt{google/gemma-4-12B-it} model and uses deterministic decoding during inference. The generated outputs are normalized to extract the hallucination label and the selected option. The system achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.928 and a label accuracy of 0.935 for hallucination detection, together with an option accuracy of 0.895 for answer selection. These results yield a combined score of 0.912, demonstrating strong performance across both stages of the task. The lower option-selection accuracy indicates that distinguishing the verified answer from plausible alternatives remains more challenging than detecting hallucinated responses.

</details>


### [192] [GPTKB 2.0: Direct Construction of Disambiguated Knowledge Bases from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03729)
*Yujia Hu,Tuan-Phong Nguyen,Simon Razniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GPTKB 2.0 是一种直接从大语言模型构建消歧知识库的方法论，通过在实体、关系和类别层面进行实时消歧，成功构建了包含超过100万消歧实体和3840万三元组的百万级LLM原生知识库，这是首个具备显式内部规范化能力的百万级LLM原生知识库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM本身不具备实体的显式表示，直接生成知识库会导致重复条目和概念混淆（conflation）问题，因此需要一种能够从LLM直接构建消歧知识库的方法。

Method: GPTKB 2.0在知识库构建过程中引入实时的实体、关系和类别消歧机制，通过精心设计在可扩展性和消歧准确性之间取得平衡，并对核心设计决策进行了系统分析，刻画了准确性、规模与成本之间的权衡。

Result: 在大规模上执行GPTKB 2.0，获得了包含超过100万消歧实体和3840万三元组的物化知识库，是首个具有显式内部规范化（对实体、关系和类别）的百万级LLM原生知识库。

Conclusion: GPTKB 2.0证明了直接从LLM构建大规模且经过消歧的知识库是可行的，标志着从传统以Wikimedia为中心的知识库构建范式向LLM原生知识库的重大转变，系统已开放于https://gptkb.org/。

Abstract: Automated Knowledge Base Construction (AKBC) is a core NLP task, and recent work proposes generating knowledge bases directly from large language models (LLMs), treating the model itself as the knowledge source. However, LLMs natively possess no representation of entities, leading to duplicate entries as well as conflations. We propose GPTKB 2.0, a methodology for constructing disambiguated KBs directly from LLMs. GPTKB 2.0 incorporates on-the-fly disambiguation of entities, relations and classes, and is meticulously designed to satisfy both scalability and disambiguation accuracy. We analyze the central design decisions and characterize the trade-offs between accuracy, scale, and cost. We execute GPTKB 2.0 at scale, obtaining a materialized KB containing over 1M disambiguated entities and 38.4M triples. This represents the first million-scale LLM-native KB with explicit internal canonicalization of entities, relations, and classes, a significant departure from prior Wikimedia-centric works. GPTKB 2.0 is available at https://gptkb.org/.

</details>


### [193] [MDLMPE: Distribution Aware Positional Encoding for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03769)
*Tong Ling,Hang Lei,Feng Xiao,Changhui Sun,Jiahang Xie,Hao Liu,Lu Liu,Yanlong Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MDLMPE，一种专为掩码扩散语言模型设计的感知token可用性分布的位置编码方法，通过在RoPE基础上引入动态掩码/揭示配置信息，在LLaDA和DREAM上的多项任务中优于传统位置编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）与自回归（AR）模型在解码时的位置上下文有本质区别：AR解码暴露连续前缀，而MDLM去噪过程中产生动态的、非连续的已揭示与掩码token配置。现有位置编码如RoPE仅捕捉序列顺序和成对位移，无法感知这种动态变化的token可用性结构，因此需要专门针对MDLM设计的位置编码方法。

Method: MDLMPE将token可用性表示为二进制序列，通过距离加权高斯核进行处理，再经余弦基投影得到分布感知的位置特征。这些特征被加到token嵌入中，并由轻量级MLP映射为角度偏移量，用于调制标准RoPE的相位。该方法使位置表示显式感知不断变化的揭示/掩码配置。

Result: 在LLaDA和DREAM上的大量实验表明，MDLMPE在监督微调、预训练、零样本评估和块扩散设置中普遍优于传统位置编码方法。消融实验进一步验证了可用性状态、高斯局部性、谱基和嵌入注入的完整组合取得了最强效果。

Conclusion: 不断演化的token可用性分布是掩码扩散语言模型中有价值的位置信号，MDLMPE是该任务中首个显式感知动态revealed/masked配置的位置编码方法，为MDLM位置编码设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) enable parallel generation and bidirectional context modeling, but their positional context differs fundamentally from that of autoregressive (AR) models. Whereas AR decoding exposes a contiguous prefix, MDLM denoising produces dynamic, non-contiguous configurations of revealed and masked tokens. Conventional positional encodings such as RoPE capture sequence order and pairwise displacement but remain insensitive to this evolving token-availability structure. To address this limitation, we propose MDLMPE, a positional encoding designed specifically for masked diffusion. To the best of our knowledge, MDLMPE is the first method to make positional representations explicitly aware of the changing revealed/masked configuration. It represents token availability as a binary sequence, applies distance-aware Gaussian weighting, and projects the resulting pattern through a cosine basis to obtain distribution-aware positional features. These features are added to token embeddings and mapped by a lightweight MLP to angular offsets that modulate the standard RoPE phases. Extensive experiments on LLaDA and DREAM demonstrate that MDLMPE generally outperforms conventional positional encoding methods across supervised fine-tuning, pretraining, zero-shot evaluation, and block-diffusion settings. Further ablations show that the complete combination of availability state, Gaussian locality, spectral basis, and embedding injection yields the strongest result. These results establish the evolving token-availability distribution as a useful positional signal for masked diffusion language models.

</details>


### [194] [Efficient Knowledge Distillation for LLMs: Offline Top-K Logits and a Fused Chunked KL Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03796)
*Bakbergen Ryskulov,Iker García-Ferrero,David Montero,David Jansen,Ali Hashemi,Jezabel R. Garcia,Antonio Tiene,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于小型语言模型蒸馏训练效率的实践研究。作者提出两大系统贡献：一是离线知识蒸馏（缓存教师模型Top-K logits）相比在线蒸馏训练损失几乎相同，但训练速度提升约29%，吞吐量最高提升41%；二是引入fused chunked KL loss，避免物化完整的词表大小logit张量，使峰值内存与序列长度线性相关，从而在单GPU上将上下文长度扩展到4倍（32,768 tokens），并支持从4K到256K tokens的内存和迭代率缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型常因延迟、成本和本地部署约束成为唯一选择，但很少从头训练，通常通过知识蒸馏（KD）从压缩模型中恢复。这个恢复步骤很大程度上决定了最终质量，但蒸馏训练成本高昂。作者旨在通过系统层面的优化使大规模蒸馏训练和大量消融实验变得负担得起。

Method: 作者提出两个核心系统优化：(1) 离线KD：一次性缓存教师模型的Top-K logits，学生仅针对缓存进行训练，移除教师模型的内存占用，实现约29%的每迭代速度提升和高达41%的吞吐量提升，而训练损失与在线蒸馏几乎一致；(2) fused chunked KL loss：采用分块融合方式计算KL损失，不物化完整的词表大小logit张量，使峰值内存线性于序列长度，消除了限制上下文长度的内存峰值，可在单GPU上训练4倍上下文长度（32,768 tokens）。作者还通过一个仅输出头的玩具基准测试隔离损失核，验证了从4K到256K tokens的扩展性，并进行了损失设计和序列打包的辅助消融实验。

Result: 离线KD与在线蒸馏训练损失几乎相同，但每迭代快约29%，单张H200 GPU上吞吐量最高提升41%；fused chunked KL loss消除了内存峰值，使单GPU可训练32,768 tokens的上下文（4倍扩展）；玩具基准测试确认损失核从4K到256K tokens的内存和迭代率扩展性良好；整体使得大规模修复训练和数百次消融实验变得可行。

Conclusion: 通过离线KD缓存和fused chunked KL loss两大系统贡献，显著降低了小型语言模型蒸馏训练的成本和内存开销，使大规模蒸馏训练以及大量消融实验变得可行。作者公开了chunked loss实现代码供社区使用。

Abstract: Small language models are often the only option for deployment under tight latency, cost, and on-premises constraints, but they are rarely trained from scratch: a compressed model is usually recovered through knowledge distillation (KD). This recovery step largely decides the final quality, yet it is expensive. We present a practitioner's study of how to make distillation training efficient, organised around two systems contributions. First, we show that offline KD (caching the teacher's top-$K$ logits once and training the student against the cache) matches online distillation at near-identical training loss while removing the teacher from memory, running about 29\% faster per iteration, and reaching up to 41\% higher throughput on a single H200 GPU. Second, we introduce a \emph{fused, chunked KL loss} that never materialises the full vocabulary-sized logit tensor, making peak memory linear in the sequence length. This removes the memory spike that otherwise caps context length and lets us train at four times the context (32{,}768 tokens) on a single GPU. A separate output-head-only toy benchmark isolates the loss kernel and confirms its memory and iteration-rate scaling from 4K to 256K tokens. Together these make large-scale healing and hundreds of ablations affordable. We also report supporting ablations on loss design and sequence packing. We release our chunked-loss implementation: https://github.com/CompactifAI/Full-Chunked-KL-Loss.

</details>


### [195] [M-GATE: Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03803)
*Tomáš Burkert,Angelika Peljak-Łapińska,David Zelený*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M-GATE基准，用于区分多语言模型的"流利度"（能否用语言完成任务）与"熟练度"（是否真正掌握语言本身）。该基准覆盖30种类型多样的语言，包含三类任务：语法错误检测、往返翻译和分词效率评估。实验发现流利度与熟练度严重脱节：模型翻译表现出色但对抗性语法任务接近随机水平；翻译质量与预训练数据比例强相关，低资源语言惩罚明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准存在根本性缺陷：它们测试模型"能否用某种语言完成某任务"，而非模型是否真正掌握这门语言本身的语法与结构，将流利度与熟练度混为一谈。这导致对多语言模型能力的高估与误判。

Method: M-GATE包含三个任务：(1) 基于语言学家人工构建并对抗性筛选的句子做语法错误检测，聚焦困难、语言特异性现象；(2) 将共享英文源文本往返翻译到29种目标语言，由经过专业标注者验证的三提供商LLM评委小组评分；(3) 补充性的分词器效率指标。共评估50余个模型的80多种配置。

Result: 流利度与熟练度严重分离：翻译良好的模型在对抗性语法项目上接近随机水平，最佳模型MCC仅0.36，且错误偏向漏报（接受不合语法文本）；翻译质量与预训练数据份额高度相关（r=0.86），低资源语言惩罚明显但随着模型迭代而收窄；推理能力稳定提升翻译但对抗性检测的提升较小甚至为负，最优配置依任务而定。

Conclusion: 当前多语言模型存在"能翻译但不懂语法"的系统性缺陷，语言熟练度与任务流利度是不可互换的能力维度。为抵抗污染，测试项目保密并持续更新公开排行榜（https://m-gate.ai），仅发布示例。

Abstract: Multilingual language models are deployed across a hundred or more languages, yet most benchmarks test whether a model can perform a task _in_ a language rather than whether it commands the language itself, conflating fluency with proficiency. We introduce M-GATE (Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency), a benchmark of linguistic proficiency spanning 30 typologically diverse languages from high- to low-resource. M-GATE comprises three tasks: grammatical error detection on linguist-crafted, adversarially selected sentences that turn on hard, language-specific phenomena; round-trip translation of shared English sources across 29 target languages, scored by a three-provider LLM judge panel validated against professional annotators; and a supplementary tokenizer-efficiency measure. We evaluate over 50 models in more than 80 configurations. Fluency and proficiency come apart sharply: models that translate competently sit near chance on the adversarial grammar items, the best reaching a Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) of only 0.36, and their errors lean systematically toward under-flagging, accepting ungrammatical text rather than raising false alarms. Translation quality closely tracks a language's share of pretraining data (r = 0.86 against log Common Crawl share), producing a steep low-resource penalty that is nonetheless narrowing with successive model releases. Enabling reasoning reliably improves translation, while its effect on error detection is smaller and for some models negative, so the best configuration is task-dependent. To resist contamination, test items are kept private behind a continuously updated public leaderboard, with illustrative examples released (https://m-gate.ai).

</details>


### [196] [VIBE: A VAD-Informed Benchmark for Entity-Centered Affective Profiling of Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03810)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Alexander Evseev,Timofei Sivoraksha,Stepan Ukolov,Mikhail Solovev,Danil Sazanakov,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VIBE基准，用于在效价-唤醒-支配（VAD）空间中刻画LLM输出对实体的情感画像。其核心是一套"测量契约"：将生成与外部评分分离，区分标量偏好、响应级VAD与目标定向VAD，并通过"情感护照"报告画像。通过三项实证支持此契约，主张实体中心情感画像应作为有记录的实践发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在描述政治人物、国家、宗教、组织、历史事件和社会群体等社会显著性目标时，会在事实内容之外编码情感框架（如目标显得有利或威胁、平静或冲突、强大或脆弱）。现有工作通过情感、好感度和情绪基准捕捉了部分空间，但没有任何工作将目标定向的VAD归因、显式评分者契约和护照式报告格式三者结合起来。该空白催生了VIBE基准。

Method: VIBE的核心是测量契约，包含三部分：将生成与外部评分分离、区分标量偏好/响应级VAD/目标定向VAD三种测量层次、通过情感护照（Affective Passport）报告画像。实证层面通过三个假设支撑：H1验证标量偏好不能涵盖唤醒与支配维度（价值维度与人类评分交叉验证rV=0.944，评分者间rV=0.954；唤醒和支配维度是单评分者方向性估计，人类标注者间仅rA=0.495、rD=0.702）；H2证明整个响应的VAD与目标定向VAD是不同契约；H3说明提示条件会改变画像，需记录上下文元数据。

Result: 三项实证结果支持测量契约：H1表明标量偏好无法替代唤醒和支配信息，且唤醒与支配维度在人类标注者间一致性较低（rA=0.495, rD=0.702），应作为方向性估计而非精确点估计；H2证实同一文本整体的情感基调可以与其中特定目标的情感画像不同；H3表明提示条件会系统性地改变情感画像，需要在每次情感报告中附带协议与上下文信息。

Conclusion: 这些结果论证了实体中心情感画像应作为有记录的实践：情感画像应附带评分者身份、覆盖范围、协议和解释限制等信息一同发布。VIBE为LLM输出的情感评测提供了更严谨、可复现的测量框架。

Abstract: Large language models routinely describe socially salient targets, including political figures, countries, religions, organizations, historical events, and social groups, encoding affective framing alongside factual content: a target may appear favorable or threatening, calm or conflictual, powerful or vulnerable. Existing work captures parts of this space through sentiment, favorability, and emotion benchmarks, but none combines target-directed VAD attribution, an explicit scorer contract, and a passport reporting format. We introduce VIBE, a benchmark for entity-centered affective profiling of LLM outputs in Valence-Arousal-Dominance (VAD) space. Its core contribution is a measurement contract: VIBE separates generation from external scoring, distinguishes scalar favorability, response-level VAD, and target-directed VAD, and reports profiles through an Affective Passport. Three empirical layers support the contract. H1 shows scalar favorability does not subsume arousal and dominance: valence findings are cross-validated (rV = 0.944 judge-human, rV = 0.954 inter-scorer); arousal and dominance are single-scorer directional estimates, not point-precise, consistent with known inter-annotator difficulty on these axes (rA = 0.495, rD = 0.702 among human annotators). H2 shows whole-response and target-directed VAD are different contracts: the same text can carry one affective tone overall while representing the named target differently. H3 is a protocol-drift diagnostic: elicitation conditions shift profiles, motivating context metadata in every affective report. These results motivate entity-centered affective profiling as a documented practice: profiles should be released with scorer identity, coverage, protocol, and interpretation limits.

</details>


### [197] [Sensitivity, Causality, and Repair Dissociate: A Layer-Wise Analysis of Perturbation Robustness and Its Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03842)
*Nathan Labiosa,David Buff,Ena Nayak,Erica Donno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了语言模型在表面扰动（拼写错误、OCR噪声、同音词）下"哪一层负责"的三种不同定义——敏感性（表示发散最大处）、因果性（恢复干净激活可修复预测处）和补偿能力（小适配器可修复损伤处），并证明这三者相互分离。在五个模型中发现两种传播机制（尖峰-抑制型和晚期累积型），敏感性与因果性呈反相关。通过级联破坏机制解释这一分离：放置于因果相关早期层的适配器会破坏完整的下游计算。层扫描实验确认了核心预测，并提供了无训练的LRD预筛选和默认最深放置规则等实用指导。此外揭示了一个方法论警告：表示稳定性损失表面上的收益在充足生成预算下会逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：当语言模型面对表面扰动的输入（如拼写错误、OCR噪声、同音词）时，"哪一层导致失败"这一问题缺乏统一的定义和明确的答案。作者希望厘清三种自然但可能不相同的操作性定义（敏感性、因果性、补偿能力），并探究它们之间的关系，从而深入理解模型内部机制并为修复策略提供指导。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 在五模型面板（Phi-3.5, Gemma-2-9B, Llama-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5-7B）上定义并测量三种层图（敏感性、因果性、补偿能力）；(2) 识别两种传播机制（spike-and-suppress与late-accumulation）；(3) 在Qwen2.5家族（1.5B至14B）上验证跨规模的一致性；(4) 提出级联破坏机制作为层图分离的解释；(5) 在四个模型上进行固定测试平台的层扫描，在GSM8K链式思维任务和多重选择对照任务上验证预测；(6) 提出无训练的LRD预筛选和默认最深放置规则等实用指导。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 三种层图（敏感性、因果性、补偿能力）相互分离；(2) 五模型面板划分为两种传播机制：spike-and-suppress（Phi-3.5, Gemma-2-9B）和late-accumulation（Llama-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5-7B）；(3) 在通过80%身份补丁门槛的两个模型上，敏感性与因果性呈强反相关（rho=-0.72至-0.88）；(4) 跨规模实验（Qwen2.5 1.5B-14B）显示late-accumulation特征随规模单调增强，并在第二个模型家族中获得佐证；(5) 层扫描确认了级联破坏预测——因果标记的位置是所有可裁决模型上最具破坏性的适配器窗口，在链式思维GSM8K上该结果显著，在多重选择对照上仅符号一致且大幅衰减；(6) 表示稳定性损失的表面收益在充足生成预算下逆转（截断的链式思维曾被计为空），构成方法论警告。

Conclusion: 结论：语言模型在表面扰动失败中"哪一层负责"取决于所问的问题——三种操作性定义（敏感性、因果性、补偿能力）彼此分离。级联破坏机制解释了为何因果标记的早期层实际上是最差的适配器放置位置：在这些层放置适配器会破坏未受损的完整下游计算。研究提供实用指导（无训练的LRD预筛选、默认最深放置规则），并强调了一个重要的方法论警示：在链式思维任务上评估任何干预措施时，必须保证充足的生成预算，否则截断输出可能被错误计为空，导致虚假的收益信号。

Abstract: When a language model fails on surface-perturbed input (typos, OCR noise, homophones), "which layer is responsible" has three natural operationalizations: where representations diverge most (sensitivity), where restoring clean activations recovers the prediction (causality), and where a small adapter can repair the damage (compensatory capacity) - and we show these three layer maps dissociate. Across a five-model panel we identify two propagation regimes - spike-and-suppress (Phi-3.5, Gemma-2-9B) and late-accumulation (Llama-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5-7B) - and on the two models meeting an 80% identity-patch gate, sensitivity and causality are anti-correlated (rho = -0.72 to -0.88). Within-family scaling on Qwen2.5 (1.5B to 14B) shows the late-accumulation signature strengthening monotonically with scale, corroborated on a second family. We propose cascade disruption as the mechanism behind the dissociation: adapters placed at causally implicated early layers break intact downstream computation, making diagnostic-flagged sites the worst adapter placements. A fixed-harness layer sweep across four models (3.8-8B) confirms the core prediction on chain-of-thought GSM8K - the flagged sites are the most damaging adapter windows on every adjudicable model - and is sign-consistent but strongly attenuated on a multiple-choice control, consistent with damage that compounds with generation length. The sweep yields practical guidance: a training-free LRD pre-screen and a default-deepest placement rule, though absolute gains over no-adapter baselines remain small. Finally, apparent gains from a representation-stability loss reverse under an adequate generation budget - truncated chain-of-thought had been scored as empty - a methodological warning for any intervention evaluated on chain-of-thought tasks.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Representational Similarity: Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain for Generative Plagiarism Detection and Candidate Source Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03859)
*Peijia Guo,Wenxuan Xie,ZiGuang Li,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCDG框架，一种基于描述长度增益的、无需训练的方向性检测方法，用于检测大规模语言模型生成的抄袭内容。通过在有无候选源$S$的条件下对比冻结语言模型对可疑文档$P$的描述长度，计算token级对数似然增益来衡量源提供的增量预测证据。在PAN CLEF基准上，SCDG在成对基准测试中达到0.94 F1，在多源检索任务中达到0.83 nDCG@10和0.96 Recall@100，全面超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式抄袭检测面临两大挑战：一是基于LLM文本检测的方法仅关注AI参与程度而非源复用；二是基于相似度的方法在大量改写和多源综合场景下失效。这些空白使得生成式抄袭检测成为一个未被充分探索且尚未解决的问题。

Method: SCDG基于概率预测的描述长度视角，对比冻结语言模型在有/无候选源$S$两个条件下对可疑文档$P$的描述长度（编码长度）。两者对比得到token级别的对数似然增益，该增益度量$S$所提供的增量预测证据。该方法是无训练的、方向性的（从源到文档），并可分解到token级别。

Result: 在PAN 2025衍生成对基准上，SCDG达到0.92精确率、0.97召回率和0.94 F1，超越所有基线；在PAN 2026多源检索任务上达到0.83 nDCG@10和0.96 Recall@100，同样超越所有基线。在同主题同事件的Multi-News测试中，校准后的SCDG分类器仅对0.125%的配对判定为源复用，展示了在主题重叠场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SCDG被确立为一种统一的、可token分解的源特定内容复用信号，能在大量转换条件下有效检测源复用，为生成式抄袭检测提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) pose challenges to academic integrity and peer review. Yet generative plagiarism detection remains an underexplored and largely unresolved challenge. Prior work on LLM-generated-text detection targets AI involvement, which may be permissible, rather than source reuse, while similarity-based methods struggle after extensive rewriting and multi-source synthesis. Motivated by the description-length view of probabilistic prediction, in which relevant side information can reduce a target sequence's code length, we introduce Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain (SCDG), a directional, training-free framework that contrasts a frozen language model's description length of a suspicious document $P$ with and without a candidate source $S$. This contrast yields token-level log-likelihood gains that measure the incremental predictive evidence supplied by $S$. We evaluate SCDG on the PAN at CLEF benchmarks for generative plagiarism. On a PAN 2025-derived pairwise benchmark, SCDG achieves 0.92 Precision, 0.97 Recall, and 0.94 F1, outperforming all baselines; on PAN 2026's multi-source retrieval task, it reaches 0.83 nDCG@10 and 0.96 Recall@100, surpassing all baselines. On a same-topic, same-event Multi-News test, the calibrated gain-distribution SCDG classifier predicts source reuse for only $0.125\%$ of pairs, supporting robustness to topical overlap under this evaluation protocol. These results establish SCDG as a unified and token-decomposable signal for source-specific content reuse under extensive transformation.

</details>


### [199] [SciRet: A Compute-Aware Empirical Study of Retrieval and Reranking for Scientific RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03860)
*Kaysarul Anas Apurba,Md. Hasibul Hasan,Rofiqul Alam Shehab,Asab Azad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SciRet，一个针对CORD-19科学问答的检索增强生成（RAG）计算感知实证研究。研究在三种语料规模（1K/5K/15K论文）下评估固定科学RAG流水线，发现混合检索比单一稀疏或稠密检索更稳健（Recall@10达到1.000），MS MARCO训练的交叉编码器重排序器因领域不匹配反而降低精度，生成忠实度随语料规模增加而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG研究多聚焦于提出新模型，缺乏对科学领域RAG组件在可扩展语料规模下的系统性和可复现性评估。作者希望通过固定流水线的实证研究，为科学问答场景下不同检索策略、重排序器和语料规模的影响提供受控对比证据。

Method: 构建固定科学RAG流水线，包含句子窗口分块、BM25稀疏检索、BGE-M3稠密检索、倒数排名融合（RRF）、可选的交叉编码器重排序及基于证据的答案生成。在1K/5K/15K三种语料规模下对比稀疏、稠密和混合检索策略，并使用RAGAS评估生成忠实度。检索标签采用混合系统的伪相关性标注。

Result: 混合检索在1K和15K规模下达到Recall@10为1.000，优于单一检索策略；MS MARCO预训练的交叉编码器在科学语料上降低了精度，体现领域不匹配的负面影响；生成忠实度（RAGAS）随语料规模增大而提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明混合检索是科学RAG的稳健默认选择，但跨领域预训练的重排序模型需要谨慎使用；语料规模的扩大有助于提升生成忠实度。由于评估标签来自混合系统本身，结果定位为受控对比证据而非基准声明，并开放代码与索引支持复现。

Abstract: We introduce SciRet, a compute-aware empirical study of retrieval-augmented generation for scientific question answering over CORD-19. Rather than proposing a new model, we evaluate a fixed scientific RAG pipeline across three corpus scales: 1,034 chunks (1K papers), 5,160 chunks (5K papers), and 15,480 chunks (15K papers). The pipeline combines sentence-window chunking, BM25, BGE-M3 dense retrieval, reciprocal rank fusion, optional cross-encoder reranking, and grounded answer generation. Across these settings, hybrid retrieval is more robust than either sparse-only or dense-only retrieval in our setting, reaching Recall@10 of 1.000 at 1K and 15K. In contrast, an MS MARCO-trained cross-encoder reranker reduces precision on the scientific corpus, suggesting that domain mismatch can outweigh the benefits of stronger query-passage interaction. Generation faithfulness measured with RAGAS increases with corpus scale in our setup. Retrieval evaluation uses pseudo-relevance labels derived from the hybrid system, so we treat the results as controlled comparative evidence rather than a benchmark claim. We release code, indexes, and evaluation outputs to support replication and follow-up studies.

</details>


### [200] [MultiGlobeQA: A Multilingual and Globally Diverse Benchmark for Geospatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03882)
*Martin Böckling,Elizaveta Nosova,Heiko Paulheim,Andreea Iana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiGlobeQA，一个包含46,060个问答对的多语言地理空间推理基准，覆盖201个国家和地区、14种空间函数家族、15种答案格式及17种语言。实验发现LLM在网格索引和形状计算任务上表现最差，拓扑关系和方向任务表现最佳；即使提供黄金事实，性能仍低于三分之二，表明计算瓶颈而非知识获取是主要限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间推理基准存在局限性：数据为合成或小规模、主要限于单语、对地理覆盖的控制有限。这导致无法全面定位和评估LLM在地理空间推理中的失败模式，亟需一个大规模、多语言、覆盖面广的基准来系统评估LLM的地理推理能力。

Method: 构建MultiGlobeQA基准，包含46,060个问答对，覆盖14种空间函数家族和15种答案格式，基于三个知识图谱生成可执行的真实答案。通过收入和密度分层抽样覆盖201个国家和领土，同时生成英语及16种高/低资源语言的平行问题。在参数化（零样本/少样本）、推理（CoT）和智能体（检索/工具使用）三种设置下评估多个LLM。

Result: LLM在网格索引和形状计算任务上表现崩塌，而在拓扑关系和方向任务上表现最佳。检索和工具使用带来显著提升，但即使用户提供黄金事实，性能仍低于三分之二，表明计算能力而非知识获取是主要瓶颈。模型在低收入地区表现欠佳，且提供黄金事实反而扩大了这一地区差距。

Conclusion: 地理空间推理的主要瓶颈在于LLM的几何与拓扑计算能力，而非地理知识储备。即使提供正确的空间事实，模型仍难以完成所需的计算任务。不同空间函数家族的难度差异显著，需要针对性的模型改进。此外，低收入地区的性能差距提示基准和模型都需关注地理覆盖的公平性。

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning, i.e., computing distances, containment, and other spatial relations over real-world entities, is central to navigation and logistics, yet large language models (LLMs) struggle with the required geometric and topological computation despite storing considerable geographic knowledge. Existing benchmarks localize these failures only partially: they are synthetic or smallscale, largely monolingual, and offer limited control over geographic coverage. We introduce MultiGlobeQA, a multilingual benchmark of 46,060 question-answer pairs spanning 14 spatial-function families and 15 answer formats, with execution-based ground truth over three knowledge graphs. It covers 201 countries and territories via income- and density-stratified sampling, with parallel questions in English and 16 additional high- and low-resource languages. Across parametric, reasoning, and agentic settings, LLMs collapse on tasks requiring grid indexing and shape computation, while topological relations and directions fare best. Retrieval and tool use yield considerable gains, yet performance plateaus below two thirds even when gold facts are supplied, indicating that computation, not access to knowledge, is the bottleneck. Models also underperform on low-income regions, a gap that gold facts widen rather than close.

</details>


### [201] [DS@GT-ARC at eRisk 2026 Task 3: Sparse, Semantic, and LLM Reranking for ADHD Symptom Sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03883)
*David Guecha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了作者团队在eRisk 2026任务3（ADHD症状句子排序）中的提交系统。由于该任务未提供标注训练数据，团队采用零样本方法，构建了结合BM25稀疏检索、实证感知重打分、嵌入重排序、查询原型扩展和LLM重排序的分阶段检索管线，其中LLM重排序器取得了最佳官方分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eRisk 2026任务3是首次举办的ADHD症状句子排序任务，要求系统将Reddit候选句子按与ASRS-v1.1量表中18个症状的相关性进行排序。由于没有发布标注训练数据，需要探索无需监督训练的零样本或弱引导检索方案。

Method: 采用分阶段检索设计：第一阶段使用BM25在大规模候选中进行稀疏检索；随后结合实证感知重打分（针对自我指涉症状报告）、基于嵌入向量的重排序、查询原型扩展（query-prototype expansion）以及基于LLM的重排序器对排名进行逐级精化。

Result: 在所有提交系统中，LLM重排序器取得了最强的官方分数，其次是原型查询扩展方案。作者团队的人工top-10分析结果与官方专家评分趋势一致。

Conclusion: 分阶段重排序（staged reranking）是一个有前景的发展方向，实验结果表明即使在没有标注数据的情况下，通过组合多种无监督/弱引导检索与重排序技术仍可获得良好的症状相关句排序效果。

Abstract: This paper describes our submissions to eRisk 2026 Task 3, ADHD Symptom Sentence Ranking. The task requires systems to rank candidate Reddit sentences according to their relevance to each of the 18 symptoms in the Adult ADHD Self-Report Scale (ASRS-v1.1). Because no annotated training data were released for this first edition of the task, we relied on zero-shot experimentation, manual validation, and unsupervised or weakly guided retrieval pipelines. Our systems combine sparse BM25 retrieval, evidence-aware rescoring for self-referential symptom reports, embedding-based reranking, query-prototype expansion, and LLM-based reranking. All submitted systems follow a staged retrieval design in which BM25 retrieves candidates at scale and semantic or LLM rerankers refine the final rankings. Among our submissions, the LLM reranker achieved the strongest official scores, followed by the prototype query-expansion run. Our manual top-10 analysis aligned with the official expert scoring trend, suggesting that staged reranking is a promising direction for further development.

</details>


### [202] [ANNOTARES: A Dataset for Extracting Logical Structures from German Statutory Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03898)
*Ronja Schwarz,Jannik Strötgen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了德语法条中法律要件（Tatbestand）与法律后果（Rechtsfolge）识别与分割的新任务，并发布了带跨度级标注的数据集ANNOTARES（涵盖三部德国法典），通过基准测试多种模型（规则、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF、BERT及LLM），证明BERT和基于LLM的方法在解析法律语言复杂句法结构上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本的自动结构分析是法律技术的基石，但提取其逻辑组成部分（法律要件与法律后果）仍是一项重大挑战，缺乏相应的数据集和系统性的方法评测。

Method: 构建了ANNOTARES数据集，包含德语法条中跨度级标注的Tatbestand-Rechtsfolge序列，覆盖三部不同的法律典，用于评估领域特定表现和跨法典泛化能力；同时基准测试了规则基线、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF、BERT变体及LLM等多种架构。

Result: BERT和基于LLM的模型在捕捉法律语言的复杂句法结构方面取得了优越的性能，优于传统机器学习方法和规则基线。

Conclusion: 作者公开数据集以促进自动化法律推理领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: The automatic structural analysis of legal texts is a cornerstone of legal technology, yet the extraction of their logical components remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we introduce the task of identifying and segmenting legal conditions (Tatbestand) and legal consequences (Rechtsfolge) within German statutory texts. To support this task, we present ANNOTARES (Annotations of Tatbestand-Rechtsfolge Sequences), a novel dataset comprising German law texts with span-level annotations. Spanning three distinct legal codes, the dataset is designed to evaluate both domain-specific performance and cross-statute generalizability. We benchmark diverse architectural approaches: a rule-based baseline, CRFs, BiLSTMs, BiLSTM-CRF, and modern Transformer-based models, including BERT variants and LLM-based methods. Our results demonstrate that BERT and LLM-based models achieve superior performance in capturing the complex syntactic structures of legal language. We release our dataset to facilitate further research in automated legal reasoning.

</details>


### [203] [Logic Before Language: Pre-pretraining on Formal Derivations Fosters Skill Acquisition and Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03930)
*Jo-Ku Cheng,Nikolaos Aletras,Marco Valentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出逻辑预预训练（Logic-PPT），通过形式化推导赋予语言模型更丰富的结构性和语言偏置，并在100B token规模下验证其能显著加速语言技能习得、改善内部表征结构并提升模型可压缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预预训练任务（如Dyck和过程式算法）依赖过于狭窄的原语，无法捕捉自然语言的表达能力；同时前期研究局限于较小的token预算，难以洞察技能涌现和表征动态。因此需要一种更原则化的初始化策略，以更丰富的结构偏置加速自然语言习得。

Method: 提出逻辑预预训练（Logic-PPT），利用形式化推导作为初始化策略。形式化推导需要抽象机制（变量绑定、量词与关系依赖连接、长上下文中谓词-论元结构组合），这些机制是自然语言的核心。作者在100B token规模下进行大规模评估。

Result: 逻辑预预训练显著加速语言模型技能习得：在语言任务上达到80%准确率时比标准初始化少消耗36B token，且优于其他预预训练基线。机制上，形式化推导诱导持久的结构重组，表现为低秩、谱集中的表征空间。该内部几何结构使模型在约33%稀疏度下剪枝后仍保持与稠密基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 逻辑预预训练是一种有效的语言模型初始化策略，不仅在token效率上大幅优于现有方法，还通过塑造特定的内部表征几何结构（低秩、谱集中）提升了模型的可压缩性，为理解和优化LM预训练提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Pre-pretraining language models (LMs) on symbolic data can accelerate and improve natural language acquisition. However, existing pre-pretraining tasks, such as Dyck and procedural algorithms, rely on narrow primitives that fail to capture the expressive capacity of natural language. Moreover, prior studies remain restricted to relatively small token budgets, offering limited insight into skill emergence and representational dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose logic pre-pretraining (Logic-PPT) as a principled initialization strategy, leveraging formal derivations to impart richer structural and linguistic biases. Formal derivations require abstract mechanisms that are central to natural language, simultaneously binding variables, connecting quantifiers and relational dependencies, and composing predicate-argument structures over long contexts. Scaling our evaluation to a 100B-token regime, logic pre-pretraining substantially accelerates skill acquisition in LMs, achieving 80\% accuracy on linguistic tasks with 36B fewer tokens than standard initialization, and outperforming alternative pre-pretraining baselines. Mechanistically, formal derivations induce persistent structural reorganization, distinctively characterized by a lower-rank, spectrally concentrated representation space. Crucially, we show that this internal geometry enables improved model compressibility via pruning, matching the dense baseline performance even at $\approx$33\% sparsity.

</details>


### [204] [HalluTruthQA-4K: A Fine-Grained Corpus and Annotation Process for Arabic Hallucination Detection and Truth Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03966)
*Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Hichem Telli,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Abdenour Hadid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HalluTruthQA-4K，一个扩展至4000条实例的阿拉伯语问答幻觉检测数据集，覆盖伊斯兰知识、历史、科学和地理四个领域，包含错误片段级标注、人工解释和层次化幻觉类型，作为HalluScoring 2026共享任务Track 2的官方数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阿拉伯语幻觉资源通常对整个回答分配二元标签（幻觉或非幻觉），但无法提供关于精确错误内容、错误原因或正确事实答案的详细信息，难以满足细粒度检测和验证的需求。

Method: 通过问题选择、受控答案生成、候选构建、专家标注、独立验证、裁决和质量控制等流程构建数据集；每个实例包含阿拉伯语问题、模型生成回答、已验证的参考答案和五个合理干扰项；幻觉回答额外标注字符级错误片段、人工撰写的解释和层次化幻觉类型，并记录标注指南、分类体系、数据格式、标注者间一致性及语料统计。

Result: 语料包含1,643条幻觉回答和2,357条非幻觉回答，共1,843个已标注的错误片段，覆盖四个知识密集型领域，并作为HalluScoring 2026共享任务Track 2的官方数据集发布。

Conclusion: HalluTruthQA-4K为阿拉伯语大规模语言模型的幻觉检测、片段级错误定位、解释生成、事实验证及事实可靠性评估提供了可再生利用的资源。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent Arabic answers while introducing factual errors that are difficult to identify and verify. Existing Arabic hallucination resources often assign a binary label to an entire response, indicating whether it is hallucinated or non-hallucinated, but provide limited information about the exact erroneous content, the reason for the error, or the correct factual answer. We present HalluTruthQA-4K, an expanded version of the HalluTruthQA resource containing 4,000 expert-curated Arabic question-answering instances across four knowledge-intensive domains: Islamic knowledge, history, science, and geography. Serving as the official dataset for Track 2 of the HalluScoring 2026 shared task, HalluTruthQA-4K extends our original corpus to 4,000 instances. Each instance pairs an Arabic question with a model-generated response, a verified reference answer, and five plausible distractors. Hallucinated responses are additionally annotated with character-level erroneous spans, human-written explanations, and hierarchical hallucination types. The corpus contains 1,643 hallucinated and 2,357 non-hallucinated responses, with 1,843 annotated erroneous spans. We describe the resource construction and annotation methodology, including question selection, controlled answer generation, candidate construction, expert annotation, independent verification, adjudication, and quality control. We also document the annotation guidelines, taxonomy, data format, inter-annotator agreement, and corpus statistics. HalluTruthQA-4K provides a reusable resource for hallucination detection, span-level error localization, explanation generation, factual verification, and the broader evaluation of factual reliability in Arabic language models.

</details>


### [205] [string2string Studio: An Interactive, In-Browser Platform for String-to-String Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03984)
*Mirac Suzgun,James Zou,Stuart M. Shieber,Dan Jurafsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍string2string Studio，一个基于浏览器的交互式字符串到字符串分析平台，集成对齐、距离、相似度、搜索、生成指标和BLAST同源搜索六大模块，核心算法以C++编译为WebAssembly在本地运行，无需安装和数据上传，性能较Python版本提升最高2500倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字符串分析工具通常需要安装、上传数据或依赖远程服务器，缺乏可解释性和可调试性，且无法在统一平台上比较不同算法在共享输入上的表现。作者希望构建一个零安装、本地运行、可检查证据的交互式平台。

Method: 采用C++实现核心算法并编译为WebAssembly，在浏览器中本地运行；系统包含六大模块（对齐、距离、相似度、搜索、生成指标、BLAST同源搜索），支持字符、词、token、行和残基多个粒度；界面展示得分及其证据（对齐结果、编辑路径、指标匹配、搜索命中、同源痕迹），并内置Learn模式提供规范化算法演示。

Result: 内部基准测试显示较Python先前版本速度提升最高2500倍；全局/局部比对速度快于通用原生C比对器；在声明设置下与独立参考结果完全一致；同源搜索的客户端blastn路径在匹配参数下与NCBI BLAST+的排名和统计高度一致。

Conclusion: string2string Studio是一个开源、免费可用的交互式分析平台，其方法具有可检查性、可调试性和可比性，适用于自然语言处理、计算生物学和数字人文等多领域的字符串分析工作流。

Abstract: We present string2string Studio, an interactive in-browser platform for string-to-string analysis across natural language processing, computational biology, and the digital humanities. The system integrates six main modules (alignment, distance, similarity, search, generation metrics, and BLAST homology search), operating at character, word, token, line, and residue levels. Its C++-based algorithms compile to WebAssembly, so core operations run locally by default without any installation or data upload. The interface reports scores with their "evidence" (alignments, edit paths, metric matches, search hits, and homology traces), making methods inspectable, debuggable, and comparable on shared inputs. Internal benchmarks show speedups of up to 2,500x over the Python predecessor, faster global/local alignment than a general-purpose native C aligner, and exact agreement with independent references under declared settings. For homology search, the scoped client-side blastn path closely matches NCBI BLAST+ rankings and statistics under matched parameters. A curated showcase and Learn mode present canonical algorithms and metrics as reusable demonstrations. string2string Studio is open-source and freely available at string2string.org.

</details>


### [206] [When Attention Goes Blind: Numerical Failure in ALiBi Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03994)
*Christopher Schröder,Lukas Gienapp,Ferdinand Schlatt,Martin Potthast,Gerhard Heyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了ALiBi位置编码的一个被忽视的失败模式：其线性偏置缩放会因浮点精度不足而下溢，导致大量注意力权重被置零，使相关注意力头部分"失明"。作者分析该问题的影响、研究四种缓解策略，并在基于ALiBi的最先进预训练模型中验证了该问题。通过148M参数解码器的预训练实验，发现这一失败模式会显著损害token检索能力，但对标准解码器基准影响甚微。对数缩放距离是最一致的改进方案，而默认ALiBi斜率在针海拾针检索中仍是强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALiBi位置编码在大语言模型中被广泛使用，但其线性偏置缩放存在一个此前未被发现的浮点精度下溢问题，会导致注意力权重大量归零，使注意力"头"部分失效。理解和解决这一隐藏缺陷对改进位置编码设计及模型训练具有重要意义。

Method: 通过数学与数值分析表征ALiBi线性偏置下溢的机制，在现有最先进预训练模型中实证检验该问题；使用148M参数解码器模型进行全面的预训练实验，将ALiBi失败模式与上下文外退化效应分离；提出四种训练时缓解策略（包括对数缩放距离等）并单独及组合评估。

Result: ALiBi失败模式可显著损害token检索性能（尤其passkey检索），但对标准解码器基准影响较小。对数缩放距离在passkey检索中带来最一致的改进；默认ALiBi斜率在针海拾针（needle-in-a-haystack）检索中仍表现出人意料地强健。

Conclusion: 尽管ALiBi存在浮点下溢问题，默认斜率配置仍是强劲基线。作者基于实验证据提供了使用ALiBi训练模型的具体建议，包括推荐在对token检索敏感的任务中采用对数缩放距离等缓解策略。

Abstract: We identify a previously overlooked failure mode of ALiBi positional encoding: its linear bias scaling underflows floating-point precision, which zeroes out a large fraction of attention weights and renders the affected attention heads partially blind. We analyze this failure mode, characterize its impact, and examine four mitigation strategies. We further demonstrate its occurrence in state-of-the-art pretrained models based on ALiBi. Comprehensive pretraining experiments with 148M-parameter decoder models help us to disentangle its effects from out-of-context degradation. We find that ALiBi's failure mode can substantially impair token retrieval while having only a minor effect on standard decoder benchmarks. We propose four training-time mitigation strategies and evaluate them individually and in combinations, finding that log-scaled distances yield the most consistent improvements in passkey retrieval. Despite this problem, default ALiBi slopes remain a surprisingly strong baseline, particularly for needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. Based on these findings we provide concrete recommendations on how to train models with ALiBi.

</details>


### [207] [PAST-Bench: Benchmarking the Foundations of Recursive Self-Improvement in Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04003)
*Shuhan Xue,Zixin Ding,Yichen Shen,Yinjie Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Yuxin Chen,Mengdi Wang,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAST-Bench基准，用于系统评估智能体是否能通过保留的跨会话经验实现递归自我改进。实验表明改进真实但不均衡，且先进度与路径证据可分离。基于此开发Hermes+框架，通过五个针对性干预提升经验增益率并提供更清晰的路径证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进要求智能体将积累的经验转化为更好的未来行为，个人AI智能体为此提供了具体场景（保留偏好、任务历史、工具例程和学习技能）。然而，保留的经验是否真的能随时间改善智能体性能从未被系统性检验，缺乏一个能够隔离该问题的评测基准。

Method: 设计PAST-Bench基准：每个智能体在匹配条件下运行有序的新会话任务序列，通过开关保留经验来隔离其效果。覆盖26个场景、204个episode，横跨记忆、程序复用、信息收集和更新四大能力维度，同时报告后期任务增益及增益是否遵循预期的保存-检索-更新路径。基于发现开发Hermes+，在智能体循环的各阶段引入五个针对性干预。

Result: 在7个基础模型和4个智能体框架上，改进是真实的但不均衡。具有相同总体增益的智能体在增益是否有预期路径证据支持方面可能差异显著。Hermes+提升了保留经验的平均增益并提供更清晰的路径证据，在需要替换过时状态的任务上改进最强，但效果仍依赖能力和模型。

Conclusion: PAST-Bench和Hermes+为研究持久智能体如何从保留经验进步到系统性地通过经验改进提供了评估与诊断基础。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires agents to turn accumulated experience into better future behavior.
